 337: /**
 338:  * Reduce a knowledge-root-relative path for display.
 339:  *
 340:  * Constraint #3: "来源路径仅显示知识根目录内相对路径，可复制但不直接暴露任意
 341:  * 绝对路径". The provider already stores paths relative to the knowledge root
 342:  * at the source, so any absolute path arriving here is suspect and is dropped
 343:  * entirely — we deliberately do NOT attempt to "recover" a tail from an
 344:  * absolute path, because that could leak an arbitrary filesystem path chosen
 345:  * by an attacker. Parent-escape sequences are dropped for the same reason.
 346:  */
 347: export function sanitizeRelativePath(value: unknown): string {
 348:     if (typeof value !== 'string') return ''
 349:     const p = value.trim()
 350:     if (!p) return ''
 351:     // Reject anything that looks absolute (posix / windows drive letter).
 352:     if (p.startsWith('/') || p.startsWith('\\')) return ''
 353:     if (/^[a-zA-Z]:[\\/]/.test(p)) return ''
 354:     // Reject parent-escape sequences that would leave the knowledge root.
 355:     if (p.startsWith('..')) return ''
 356:     if (p.includes('\\..\\') || p.includes('/../')) return ''
 357:     return p
 358: }
 359: 
 360: /** Coerce a possibly-malformed summary into a plain dict (never throws). */
 361: function asRecord(value: unknown): KnowledgeDetail {
 362:     if (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)) {
 363:         return value as KnowledgeDetail
 364:     }
 365:     return {}
 366: }
 367: 
 368: function pickLatestDate(...candidates: Array<unknown>): string | null {
 369:     let best: string | null = null
 370:     for (const c of candidates) {
 371:         if (typeof c === 'string' && c.trim() && /\d{4}/.test(c)) {
 372:             const v = c.trim()
 373:             if (best === null || v > best) best = v
 374:         }
 375:     }
 376:     return best
 377: }
 378: 
 379: /**
 380:  * Derive the knowledge evidence card from a report's top-level KB fields.
 381:  *
 382:  * Inputs are the four optional summary objects already attached to a TA
 383:  * report response (see Report interface). All inputs may be null/undefined
 384:  * for legacy reports; the function returns a stable empty card in that case.
 385:  *
 386:  * The function is total — it never throws — so the report viewer can call it
 387:  * unconditionally without wrapping in try/catch (slow query / missing KB must
 388:  * not block the report body).
 389:  */
 390: export function deriveKnowledgeEvidenceCard(params: {
 391:     localKnowledgeSummary?: Record<string, unknown> | null
 392:     halfYearFactsSummary?: Record<string, unknown> | null
 393:     halfYearFactsStatus?: string | null
 394: }): KnowledgeEvidenceCard {
 395:     const lk = asRecord(params.localKnowledgeSummary)
 396:     const hy = asRecord(params.halfYearFactsSummary)
 397:     const hyStatus = typeof params.halfYearFactsStatus === 'string' ? params.halfYearFactsStatus : ''
 398: 
 399:     const kbDisabled = lk.kb_disabled === true
 400:     const lkStatus = typeof lk.status === 'string' ? lk.status : ''
 401:     const lkHitCount = asFiniteNumber(lk.matched_count ?? lk.knowledge_hit_count)
 402:     const hyHitCount = asFiniteNumber(hy.matched_count)
 403:     const hyPeriod = typeof hy.latest_period === 'string' && hy.latest_period ? hy.latest_period : null
 404:     const hyDisclosure = typeof hy.latest_disclosure_date === 'string' && hy.latest_disclosure_date ? hy.latest_disclosure_date : null
 405: 
 406:     const latestDate = pickLatestDate(lk.updated_at, hy.updated_at, hyDisclosure)
 407: 
 408:     // Source tiers / paths are only present when the backend chose to surface
 409:     // them (currently the report summaries do not, but the helper stays
 410:     // forward-compatible). matched_pages_brief is the KB-004 shape.
 411:     const pagesLk = Array.isArray(lk.matched_pages_brief) ? (lk.matched_pages_brief as KnowledgeDetail[]) : []
 412:     const pagesHy = Array.isArray(hy.pages) ? (hy.pages as KnowledgeDetail[]) : []
 413:     const tierSet = new Set<string>()
 414:     const pathSet = new Set<string>()
 415:     let hasToBeSupplemented = false
 416:     for (const p of pagesLk) {
 417:         if (typeof p.source_quality_tier === 'string' && p.source_quality_tier) tierSet.add(p.source_quality_tier)
 418:         if (p.is_to_be_supplemented) hasToBeSupplemented = true
 419:         const rel = sanitizeRelativePath(p.rel_path ?? p.path)
 420:         if (rel) pathSet.add(rel)
 421:     }
 422:     for (const p of pagesHy) {
 423:         const rel = sanitizeRelativePath(p.rel_path ?? p.path)
 424:         if (rel) pathSet.add(rel)
 425:     }
 426:     // Also honor an explicit source_paths list if the summary carries one.
 427:     for (const sp of asStringArray(lk.source_paths)) {
 428:         const rel = sanitizeRelativePath(sp)
 429:         if (rel) pathSet.add(rel)
 430:     }
 431:     const sourceTiers = Array.from(tierSet)
 432:     const bestSourceTier = sourceTiers.length
 433:         ? sourceTiers.reduce<string | null>((best, t) => {
 434:             if (best === null) return t
 435:             return (TIER_RANK[t] ?? -1) > (TIER_RANK[best] ?? -1) ? t : best
 436:         }, null)
 437:         : null
 438: 
 439:     const hasConflict = hy.has_conflict === true || hyStatus === 'CONFLICT'
 440:     const hasStale =
 441:         lkStatus === 'STALE' ||
 442:         hy.has_stale === true ||
 443:         hyStatus === 'STALE' ||
 444:         (asFiniteNumber(lk.stale_hit_count ?? lk.stale_mention_count) > 0 && asFiniteNumber(lk.fresh_hit_count) === 0)
 445:     const isLowConfidence = lkStatus === 'LOW_CONFIDENCE' || hyStatus === 'LOW_CONFIDENCE'
 446: 
 447:     // ── Freshness badge (priority: disabled > conflict > stale > no_data > fresh)
 448:     let freshness: KnowledgeFreshness = 'fresh'
 449:     if (kbDisabled) freshness = 'disabled'
 450:     else if (hasConflict) freshness = 'conflict'
 451:     else if (hasStale) freshness = 'stale'
 452:     else if (lkHitCount === 0 && hyHitCount === 0) freshness = 'no_data'
 453: 
 454:     // ── Gap code (priority order matters — most actionable first)
 455:     const hasAnyLkField =
 456:         lkStatus !== '' || 'matched_count' in lk || 'updated_at' in lk || 'kb_disabled' in lk
 457:     let gapCode: KnowledgeGapCode
 458:     let gapExplanation: string
 459:     if (kbDisabled) {
 460:         gapCode = 'disabled'
 461:         gapExplanation = DISABLED_HEADLINE
 462:     } else if (!hasAnyLkField && hyStatus === '' && !('matched_count' in hy)) {
 463:         // Truly legacy report — none of the KB fields were ever attached.
 464:         gapCode = 'missing_source'
 465:         gapExplanation = MISSING_SOURCE_HEADLINE
 466:     } else if (hasConflict) {
 467:         gapCode = 'conflict'
 468:         gapExplanation = CONFLICT_HEADLINE
 469:     } else if (hasStale) {
 470:         gapCode = 'stale'
 471:         gapExplanation = STALE_HEADLINE
 472:     } else if (hasToBeSupplemented || isLowConfidence) {
 473:         // LOW_CONFIDENCE / is_to_be_supplemented signal that raw research
 474:         // exists but the wiki page is a stub — pending Tree Work digestion.
 475:         gapCode = 'pending_tree_work'
 476:         gapExplanation = PENDING_TREE_WORK_HEADLINE
 477:     } else if (lkHitCount === 0 && hyHitCount === 0) {
 478:         gapCode = 'no_hit'
 479:         gapExplanation = NO_HIT_HEADLINE
 480:     } else {
 481:         gapCode = 'none'
 482:         gapExplanation = OK_HEADLINE
 483:     }
 484: 
 485:     const hasAnySignal =
 486:         hasAnyLkField ||
 487:         hyStatus !== '' ||
 488:         'matched_count' in hy ||
 489:         kbDisabled
 490: 
 491:     return {
 492:         hasAnySignal,
 493:         localKnowledgeHitCount: lkHitCount,
 494:         halfYearHitCount: hyHitCount,
 495:         halfYearPeriod: hyPeriod,
 496:         halfYearDisclosureDate: hyDisclosure,
 497:         latestDate,
 498:         bestSourceTier,
 499:         sourceTiers,
 500:         sourcePaths: Array.from(pathSet),
 501:         freshness,
 502:         gapCode,
 503:         gapExplanation,
 504:     }
 505: }

   445	# [HY-004] half_year_report_block
   446	def _cached_half_year_facts_entry(
   447	    result_data: Dict[str, Any],
   448	) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
   449	    """Return ``metadata.raw_evidence.half_year_facts`` if present.
   450	
   451	    Mirrors :func:`_cached_local_knowledge_entry` so the HY-004 attach step can
   452	    reuse the data collector payload instead of re-parsing the wiki on every
   453	    report read.
   454	    """
   455	    metadata = result_data.get("metadata")
   456	    if not isinstance(metadata, dict):
   457	        return None
   458	    raw_ev = metadata.get("raw_evidence")
   459	    if not isinstance(raw_ev, dict):
   460	        return None
   461	    entry = raw_ev.get("half_year_facts")
   462	    return entry if isinstance(entry, dict) else None

   464	
   465	def attach_report_half_year_facts(
   466	    result_data: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
   467	    symbol: Optional[str] = None,
   468	) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
   469	    """Attach [HY-004] half-year facts block to report result_data.
   470	
   471	    Adds the "半年报事实对照" section to TA reports. Strictly read-only with
   472	    respect to the strong action gate: this function MUST NOT alter
   473	    ``decision`` / ``execution_action`` / ``action_label`` /
   474	    ``research_direction`` / ``wait_reason_codes`` / ``data_blockers``.
   475	    Half-year facts serve as background evidence only and never substitute for
   476	    missing market/fund/news data (see REPORT-UX-004 isolation contract).
   477	
   478	    Priority (same shape as :func:`attach_report_local_knowledge`):
   479	      1. Reuse existing ``metadata.raw_evidence.half_year_facts`` payload when
   480	         the data collector already produced one (avoids re-parsing the wiki
   481	         for every report read).
   482	      2. Otherwise re-query by ``symbol`` (for legacy rows that predate HY-004
   483	         or runs without data_collector).
   484	      3. Skip silently when no symbol and no cached entry are available.
   485	
   486	    Output keys (all backward-compatible; old reports simply get None/empty):
   487	      - ``half_year_facts_block``: Markdown string. Empty when no half-year
   488	        page hit so the UI can hide the section.
   489	      - ``half_year_facts_summary``: dict with structured fields the frontend
   490	        can render without re-parsing markdown (status / data_status /
   491	        matched_count / latest_period / latest_disclosure_date / sections /
   492	        has_conflict / has_stale / has_opinion_only).
   493	      - ``half_year_facts_status``: short string (``HAS_FACTS`` / ``NO_DATA`` /
   494	        ``STALE`` / ``LOW_CONFIDENCE`` / ``CONFLICT`` / ``FAILED``) for
   495	        quick filtering. ``NO_DATA`` is a *gap*, not a failure — the report
   496	        must still surface the original ``wait_reason_codes``.
   497	    """
   498	    if not isinstance(result_data, dict):
   499	        return result_data
   500	    try:
   501	        from tradingagents.dataflows.half_year_facts_provider import (
   502	            HalfYearFactsQueryResult,
   503	            default_knowledge_root as _hy_default_root,
   504	            query_half_year_facts as _hy_query,
   505	            render_half_year_facts_block as _hy_render,
   506	        )
   507	
   508	        # 1. Reuse cached raw_evidence entry (preferred path).
   509	        cached_entry = _cached_half_year_facts_entry(result_data)
   510	        result_obj: Optional[HalfYearFactsQueryResult] = None
   511	        if isinstance(cached_entry, dict):
   512	            payload = cached_entry.get("raw")
   513	            if isinstance(payload, dict):
   514	                result_obj = HalfYearFactsQueryResult.from_dict(payload)
   515	
   516	        # 2. Re-query for legacy rows when no usable cache.
   517	        if result_obj is None and symbol:
   518	            resolved_symbol = str(symbol).strip()
   519	            if resolved_symbol:
   520	                try:
   521	                    result_obj = _hy_query(
   522	                        _hy_default_root(), symbol=resolved_symbol
   523	                    )
   524	                except Exception as exc:
   525	                    logger.warning(
   526	                        "HY-004 half_year_facts re-query failed for %s: %s",
   527	                        resolved_symbol,
   528	                        exc,
   529	                    )
   530	                    result_obj = None
   531	
   532	        if result_obj is None:
   533	            return result_data
   534	
   535	        # Build the short status string for top-level filtering.
   536	        status_short = _half_year_status_to_short(
   537	            result_obj.status,
   538	            result_obj.data_status,
   539	        )
   540	
   541	        enriched = dict(result_data)
   542	        enriched["half_year_facts_block"] = _hy_render(result_obj)
   543	        enriched["half_year_facts_summary"] = _build_half_year_facts_summary(
   544	            result_obj
   545	        )
   546	        enriched["half_year_facts_status"] = status_short
   547	
   548	        return enriched
   549	    except Exception as exc:
   550	        logger.warning("HY-004 half_year_facts attachment failed: %s", exc)
   551	        return result_data
   552	
   553	
   554	def _half_year_status_to_short(status: str, data_status: str) -> str:
   555	    """Map the provider status/data_status pair to a short filter token.
   556	
   557	    ``NO_DATA`` represents a *gap* (no half-year pages matched) and must NOT
   558	    be confused with ``FAILED`` (provider exception / unreadable KB). This
   559	    distinction is the core of the HY-004 acceptance criterion: "无半年报事实
   560	    时显示缺口，不误判 failed".
   561	    """
   562	    from tradingagents.dataflows.half_year_facts_provider import DATA_CONFLICT
   563	    from tradingagents.dataflows.local_knowledge_provider import (
   564	        STATUS_FAILED,
   565	        STATUS_HAS_DATA,
   566	        STATUS_LOW_CONFIDENCE,
   567	        STATUS_NORMAL_NO_DATA,
   568	        STATUS_STALE,
   569	    )
   570	
   571	    if status == STATUS_FAILED:
   572	        return "FAILED"
   573	    if status == STATUS_NORMAL_NO_DATA:
   574	        return "NO_DATA"
   575	    if status == STATUS_STALE:
   576	        return "STALE"
   577	    if status == STATUS_LOW_CONFIDENCE:
   578	        return "LOW_CONFIDENCE"
   579	    if data_status == DATA_CONFLICT:
   580	        return "CONFLICT"
   581	    if status == STATUS_HAS_DATA:
   582	        return "HAS_FACTS"
   583	    return "NO_DATA"
   584	
   585	
   586	def _build_half_year_facts_summary(
   587	    result: Any,
   588	) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   589	    """Build the structured ``half_year_facts_summary`` dict.
   590	
   591	    The summary exposes the four required sub-sections (事实 / 管理层表述 /
   592	    待验证事项 / 风险) so the frontend can render distinct cards without
   593	    re-parsing the markdown block. Each sub-section carries an
   594	    ``available`` flag, a ``count`` and a short ``preview`` list.
   595	    """
   596	    from tradingagents.dataflows.half_year_facts_provider import (
   597	        DATA_CONFLICT,
   598	        DATA_MISSING_FACTS,
   599	        DATA_MISSING_PERIOD,
   600	    )
   601	
   602	    pages = list(getattr(result, "pages", []) or [])
   603	    # 事实：聚合 financial_facts raw（每页前 3 条，全局去重保序）。
   604	    facts_preview: List[str] = []
   605	    commentary_preview: List[str] = []
   606	    needs_verification_preview: List[str] = []
   607	    risks_preview: List[str] = []
   608	
   609	    has_conflict = False
   610	    has_stale = False
   611	    has_opinion_only = False
   612	    has_missing_period = False
   613	    has_missing_facts = False
   614	
   615	    for p in pages:
   616	        if getattr(p, "data_status", None) == DATA_CONFLICT:
   617	            has_conflict = True
   618	        if getattr(p, "is_stale", False):
   619	            has_stale = True
   620	        if getattr(p, "is_opinion_only", False):
   621	            has_opinion_only = True
   622	        if getattr(p, "data_status", None) == DATA_MISSING_PERIOD:
   623	            has_missing_period = True
   624	        if getattr(p, "data_status", None) == DATA_MISSING_FACTS:
   625	            has_missing_facts = True
   626	
   627	        # 事实：financial_facts raw。
   628	        for m in (getattr(p, "financial_facts", None) or [])[:3]:
   629	            raw = getattr(m, "raw", None)
   630	            if raw and raw not in facts_preview:
   631	                facts_preview.append(raw)
   632	        # 管理层表述。
   633	        for mc in (getattr(p, "management_commentary", None) or [])[:2]:
   634	            if mc and mc not in commentary_preview:
   635	                commentary_preview.append(mc)
   636	        # 待验证事项：前瞻指引（公司指引，半事实）+ 事实冲突 + 缺字段。
   637	        for fg in (getattr(p, "forward_guidance", None) or [])[:2]:
   638	            if fg and fg not in needs_verification_preview:
   639	                needs_verification_preview.append(fg)
   640	        if getattr(p, "data_status", None) == DATA_CONFLICT and getattr(
   641	            p, "conflict_detail", None
   642	        ):
   643	            cd = p.conflict_detail
   644	            if cd and cd not in needs_verification_preview:
   645	                needs_verification_preview.append(cd)
   646	        missing_fields = list(getattr(p, "missing_fields", None) or [])
   647	        if missing_fields:
   648	            tag = "缺字段需复核：" + ",".join(missing_fields)
   649	            if tag not in needs_verification_preview:
   650	                needs_verification_preview.append(tag)

   831	    name: Optional[str] = None
   832	    trade_date: str
   833	    status: Literal["pending", "running", "completed", "failed"] = "completed"
   834	    error: Optional[str] = None
   835	    decision: Optional[str]
   836	    direction: Optional[str]
   837	    research_direction: Optional[str] = None
   838	    execution_action: Optional[str] = None
   839	    action_label: Optional[str] = None

   851	    data_blockers: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None
   852	    data_blocker_summary: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None

   855	    wait_reason_labels: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None
   856	    # [KB-011] knowledge_contract_ui — KB fields surfaced at top level so the
   857	    # frontend can render "本地知识补充 / 研报关注度" without digging into
   858	    # result_data. These are attached by ``_attach_report_data_blockers_for_response``

   863	    # [KB-008] research attention — flat fields mirrored from result_data.

   868	    # [HY-004] half_year_report_block — surfaced at top level so the frontend
   869	    # can render "半年报事实对照" without digging into result_data. Attached
   870	    # by ``_attach_report_data_blockers_for_response``; read-only with
   871	    # respect to the strong action gate.
   872	    half_year_facts_block: Optional[str] = None
   873	    half_year_facts_summary: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
   874	    half_year_facts_status: Optional[str] = None
   875	    # [PLAYBOOK-001] lifecycle_contract — optional playbook stage + summary
   876	    # dict for TA report passthrough. Populated best-effort from result_data
   877	    # by report_service; defaults to None/empty so old reports are unaffected.
   878	    # Unknown stages are never coerced to "hold".
   879	    playbook_stage: Optional[str] = None
   880	    playbook_summary: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
   881	
   882	    model_config = {"from_attributes": True}
   883	
   884	    @field_serializer("created_at", "updated_at", when_used="json")
   885	    def serialize_report_datetimes(self, value: Optional[datetime]) -> Optional[str]:
   886	        return _serialize_datetime_utc(value)

   906

   908	    on detail responses until they have a persisted storage column, instead of
   909	    turning a history list into an N+1 detail fetch.

   911

   929	    analyst_traces: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None
   930	    created_at: Optional[datetime] = None
   931	    updated_at: Optional[datetime] = None

   933	    scheduled_running_count: Optional[int] = None
   934	    scheduled_concurrency_limit: Optional[int] = None
   935	    data_blockers: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None

   706	def extract_structured_data(
   707	    final_trade_decision: str,
   708	    fundamentals_report: str = "",
   709	    config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
   710	) -> Optional[StructuredReport]:
   711	    """Use LLM structured output to extract key data from report text."""
   712	    if not final_trade_decision:
   713	        return None
   714	    if config is None:
   715	        from tradingagents.default_config import DEFAULT_CONFIG
   716	        config = DEFAULT_CONFIG
   717	
   718	    try:
   719	        from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage
   720	        from tradingagents.llm_clients import create_llm_client
   721	
   722	        client = create_llm_client(
   723	            provider=config.get("llm_provider", "openai"),
   724	            model=config.get("quick_think_llm", "gpt-4o-mini"),
   725	            base_url=config.get("backend_url"),
   726	            api_key=config.get("api_key"),
   727	        )
   728	        llm = client.get_llm()
   729	
   730	        prompt = (
   731	            "请从以下投资分析报告中提取结构化信息，并以 JSON 格式返回。\n\n"
   732	            f"【最终交易决策】\n{final_trade_decision[:3000]}\n\n"
   733	            f"【基本面报告摘要】\n{fundamentals_report[:1000]}\n\n"
   734	            "提取要求（请确保输出为有效的 JSON 对象，不要包裹在 markdown 代码块中）：\n"
   735	            "1. decision：决策方向关键词（BUY/SELL/HOLD 或 增持/减持/持有）\n"
   736	            "2. confidence：整体置信度（0-100整数），若文中未明确给出则根据语气判断\n"
   737	            "3. target_price / stop_loss_price：纯数字，若未提及则为 null\n"
   738	            "4. risks：最多5条主要风险，每条包含名称（15字内）、等级（high/medium/low）、一句话说明\n"
   739	            "5. key_metrics：最多6条关键财务/估值指标，每条包含名称、值（含单位）、优劣（good/neutral/bad）。"
   740	            "**必须严格执行优劣判断，不要全部标neutral**："
   741	            "ROE>15%为good，<8%为bad；营收增速>20%为good，<5%为bad；"
   742	            "资产负债率<40%为good，>70%为bad，60%左右为neutral；"
   743	            "经营现金流净额为正且>净利润50%为good，为负为bad；"
   744	            "净利润为正且增长为good，下降为bad；数据缺失的指标必须标bad并在value中注明'数据缺失'。"
   745	            "每个指标都必须独立判断，至少要有1个good和1个bad（如果数据支持）。"
   746	        )
   747	
   748	        response = llm.invoke([HumanMessage(content=prompt)])
   749	        raw = response.content if hasattr(response, "content") else str(response)
   750	        parsed = json_repair.loads(raw)
   751	        result = StructuredReport(**parsed)
   752	        if result.confidence is not None and not (0 <= result.confidence <= 100):
   753	            result.confidence = None
   754	        return result
   755	    except Exception as e:
   756	        logger.warning(f"LLM structured extraction failed: {e}")
   757	        if 'raw' in locals():
   758	            logger.warning(f"Raw LLM output:\n{raw}")
   759	        return None

   206

   237	
   238	    @classmethod
   239	    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> "LocalKnowledgeQueryResult":
   240	        matched = [
   241	            LocalKnowledgeMatch.from_dict(d)
   242	            for d in (data.get("matched_pages") or [])
   243	            if isinstance(d, dict)
   244	        ]
   245	        return cls(
  1120	      - ``fresh_hit_count``：fresh 命中页数。
  1121	      - ``stale_hit_count``：仅 stale 命中页数。
  1122	      - ``low_confidence_hit_count``：低置信/待补充/弱来源命中页数。
  1123	      - ``weak_source_hit_count``：弱来源命中页数（media/user_note/unknown）。
  1124	      - ``has_hit``：是否有任何 fresh 命中。
  1125	      - ``status``：透传查询状态（``HAS_DATA`` / ``NORMAL_NO_DATA`` / …）。
  1126	      - ``confidence``：透传 ``result.confidence``。
  1127	      - ``updated_at``：透传 ``result.updated_at``。
  1128	      - ``themes``：去重主题列表（前 10 条）。
  1129	      - ``risks``：风险提示列表（前 5 条）。
  1130	      - ``matched_pages_brief``：命中页精简列表，便于前端展示。
  1131	      - ``errors``：查询失败 / 解析失败原因（前 5 条），便于前端排障。
  1132	      - ``local_knowledge_summary``：一句话可读摘要，含负面信息。
  1133	
  1134	    参数:
  1135	        result: 已计算的 :class:`LocalKnowledgeQueryResult`，或 ``None``。
  1136	            传 ``None`` 时返回空命中结构（score=0、hit_count=0），调用方据此
  1137	            渲染 NORMAL_NO_DATA 语义。
  1138	
  1139	    返回:
  1140	        上述字段的扁平字典。永远不会抛异常。
  1141	    """
  1142	    empty: Dict[str, Any] = {
  1143	        "local_knowledge_score": 0.0,
  1144	        "knowledge_hit_count": 0,
  1145	        "fresh_hit_count": 0,
  1146	        "stale_hit_count": 0,
  1147	        "low_confidence_hit_count": 0,
  1148	        "weak_source_hit_count": 0,
  1149	        "has_hit": False,
  1150	        "status": STATUS_NORMAL_NO_DATA,
  1151	        "confidence": "low",
  1152	        "updated_at": None,
  1153	        "themes": [],
  1154	        "risks": [],
  1155	        "matched_pages_brief": [],
  1156	        "errors": [],
  1157	        "local_knowledge_summary": "",
  1158	    }
  1159	    if result is None or not isinstance(result, LocalKnowledgeQueryResult):
  1160	        return empty
  1161	
  1162	    matched = result.matched_pages or []
  1163	    if not matched:
  1164	        # 显式保留 status / confidence，方便调用方区分 NORMAL_NO_DATA / FAILED。
  1165	        empty["status"] = result.status
  1166	        empty["confidence"] = result.confidence
  1167	        empty["errors"] = list(result.errors or [])[:5]
  1168	        if result.status == STATUS_HAS_DATA:
  1169	            # 防御：状态为 HAS_DATA 但 matched 为空时仍视为无命中。
  1170	            empty["status"] = STATUS_NORMAL_NO_DATA
  1171	        elif result.status == STATUS_FAILED:
  1172	            empty["local_knowledge_summary"] = "本地知识查询失败，不参与命中分计算。"
  1173	        return empty
  1174	
  1175	    fresh_count = 0
  1176	    stale_count = 0
  1177	    low_count = 0
  1178	    weak_source_count = 0
  1179	    total_score = 0.0
  1180	    briefs: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
  1181	    for m in matched:
  1182	        is_weak_source = _is_weak_citation_match(m)
  1183	        if m.is_stale and not (
  1184	            m.is_low_confidence or m.is_to_be_supplemented or is_weak_source
  1185	        ):
  1186	            stale_count += 1
  1187	        elif m.is_low_confidence or m.is_to_be_supplemented or is_weak_source:
  1188	            low_count += 1
  1189	            if is_weak_source:
  1190	                weak_source_count += 1
  1191	        else:
  1192	            fresh_count += 1
  1193	        total_score += _page_score(m)
  1194	        briefs.append({
  1195	            "rel_path": m.rel_path,
  1196	            "title": m.title,
  1197	            "page_type": m.page_type,
  1198	            "updated_at": m.updated_at,
  1199	            "confidence": m.confidence,
  1200	            "is_stale": m.is_stale,
  1201	            # 前端旧逻辑只识别 low/stale；弱来源也按低置信展示，避免当强正面。
  1202	            "is_low_confidence": m.is_low_confidence or is_weak_source,
  1203	            "is_to_be_supplemented": m.is_to_be_supplemented,
  1204	            "is_weak_source": is_weak_source,
  1205	            "summary_snippet": (m.summary or "")[:_SUMMARY_MAX_CHARS],
  1206	            "matched_by": list(m.matched_by),
  1207	            # [KB-014] citation_policy — 透传来源层级
  1208	            "source_quality_tier": m.source_quality_tier,
  1209	            "citation_confidence_weight": m.citation_confidence_weight,
  1210	        })
  1211	
  1212	    score = min(total_score, _LOCAL_KNOWLEDGE_SCORE_MAX)
  1213	    score = round(score, 2)
  1214	    has_hit = fresh_count > 0
  1215	
  1216	    summary = _render_local_knowledge_summary(
  1217	        score=score,
  1218	        hit_count=len(matched),
  1219	        fresh_count=fresh_count,
  1220	        stale_count=stale_count,
  1221	        low_count=low_count,
  1222	        weak_source_count=weak_source_count,
  1223	        has_hit=has_hit,
  1224	        status=result.status,
  1225	        themes=result.themes,
  1226	        risks=result.risks,
  1227	    )
  1228	
  1229	    return {
  1230	        "local_knowledge_score": score,
  1231	        "knowledge_hit_count": len(matched),
  1232	        "fresh_hit_count": fresh_count,
  1233	        "stale_hit_count": stale_count,
  1234	        "low_confidence_hit_count": low_count,
  1235	        "weak_source_hit_count": weak_source_count,
  1236	        "has_hit": has_hit,
  1237	        "status": result.status,
  1238	        "confidence": result.confidence,
  1239	        "updated_at": result.updated_at,
  1240	        "themes": list(result.themes)[:10],
  1241	        "risks": list(result.risks)[:_MAX_RISK_ENTRIES],
  1242	        "matched_pages_brief": briefs[:_MAX_MATCHED_PAGES],
  1243	        "errors": list(result.errors or [])[:5],
  1244	        "local_knowledge_summary": summary,
  1245	    }

   147	@dataclass
   148	class ParsedMetric:
   149	    """从 ``financial_facts`` 单条文本抽取的结构化指标。
   150	
   151	    抽取失败时 ``value`` / ``change`` 为 ``None``，但 ``raw`` 始终保留原文本。
   152	    """
   153	
   154	    metric_key: str  # revenue / net_profit / gross_margin / operating_cash_flow / other
   155	    metric_label: str  # 原始指标名（如 "营收" / "归母净利"）
   156	    raw: str  # 原始事实文本（裁剪到 _FACT_MAX_CHARS）
   157	    value: Optional[str] = None  # "150.2亿" / "25.3%"
   158	    change: Optional[str] = None  # "+30.1%" / "-2pp"
   159	
   160	    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   161	        return {
   162	            "metric_key": self.metric_key,
   163	            "metric_label": self.metric_label,
   164	            "raw": self.raw,
   165	            "value": self.value,
   166	            "change": self.change,
   167	        }

### REPORT-UX-006: TA 报告知识证据来源卡与缺口解释（P2）
- **描述**：基于现有 `local_knowledge_summary` 与 `half_year_facts_summary`，在 TA 报告中清晰显示“用了哪些本地资料、资料是否过期/冲突、还缺什么”，不等待新的聚合 API。
- **优先级**：P2
- **状态**：in_progress — claimed REPORT-UX-006-20260713-234106
- **前置条件**：KB-011、HY-004、REPORT-UX-005 完成。
- **执行约束**：
  - 复用 ReportViewer，不新增重型页面；不展示长正文。
  - 来源状态不得映射成买卖动作，不改变 `decision/execution_action/action_label`。
  - 慢查询或缺知识库不阻塞报告主体。
- **实现要点**：
  1. 增加轻量来源卡：命中文档数、来源等级、最新日期、半年报期、fresh/stale/conflict/disabled。
  2. 缺口解释至少区分：无命中、缺来源、过期、事实冲突、待 Tree Work 消化、知识库禁用。
  3. 来源路径仅显示知识根目录内相对路径，可复制但不直接暴露任意绝对路径。
  4. 旧报告字段缺失时保持兼容空态。
- **验收方式**：
  - 前端 build/typecheck 通过。
  - 契约/组件测试覆盖完整、空、过期、冲突、禁用和旧报告六态。
  - 报告动作语义回放无变化。
- **代码标注要求**：`// [REPORT-UX-006] knowledge_evidence_card`

### HY-011: 半年报报告期/披露日/修订版本元数据 sanity check（P2）
- **描述**：在大量半年报进入 Tree Work 前，对报告期、披露日期、修订版本、symbol/name 和来源类型做轻量校验，阻止错期、未来日期和修订稿覆盖原稿等元数据污染。
- **优先级**：P2
- **状态**：ready
- **前置条件**：HY-001、KB-014、KB-002 完成。
- **执行约束**：
  - 只做 lint/sanity，不修改知识库文件，不调用 LLM。
  - 不猜缺失日期；缺失与非法必须分开。
  - 修订稿优先级只用于事实索引选择，不生成交易动作。
- **实现要点**：
  1. 校验 `financial_period/report_date/disclosure_date/source_quality_tier/symbol/name` 的格式与组合关系。

  4. 对修订稿保留原始/修订来源路径，不静默覆盖。
- **验收方式**：
  - fixture 覆盖合法、缺失、非法日期、未来日期、错周期、错 symbol、修订稿和多版本冲突。

## SCORE. 研究评分快照接入与四卡裁决链（2026-07-13 新增）

> 目标：知识库只提供慢变量 `research_score_snapshot`（研究证据可信度、投资逻辑质量及变化原因）；TradeFlow 继续负责实时入场时机，账户上下文负责组合适配，TA 统一执行硬门禁并映射到七阶段。禁止生成一个混合总分，也禁止知识分直接覆盖动作语义。

     8	reading files directly or fabricating context.
     9	
    10	Design contract (see docs/TASKS.md KB-006):
    11

    13	      hits the network.
    14	    * Never returns full page body text — only ``title``, ``rel_path``,
    15	      ``updated_at``, ``confidence``, ``summary_snippet`` (<=200 chars),

The patch builds and targeted tests pass, but the evidence card derives source metadata from fields that real report responses do not provide. As a result, an explicit acceptance requirement for displaying source paths/source tiers is not met.

Review comment:

- [P2] Surface real evidence sources in the card — /Users/maybee/TradingAgents-AShare/frontend/src/utils/knowledgeContract.ts:411-412
  For real report payloads, `attach_report_local_knowledge` strips the KB result down to status/count/date fields and `_build_half_year_facts_summary` does not include a `pages` array, so `lk.matched_pages_brief` and `hy.pages` are always absent for persisted reports. This means `sourcePaths`, `sourceTiers`, and `bestSourceTier` stay empty even when the report has KB hits, so the new card cannot satisfy the source-path/source-tier part of REPORT-UX-006. Please either include the brief page/tier fields in the report summaries or derive them from the existing raw evidence before returning the response.
